Abstract: Large language models show that simple autoregressive training can yield scalable and coherent generation, but extending this paradigm to speech remains challenging due to the entanglement of semantic and acoustic information. Most existing speech language models rely on text supervision, hierarchical token streams, or complex hybrid architectures, departing from the single-stream generative pretraining paradigm that has proven effective in text. In this work, we introduce WavSLM, a speech language model trained by quantizing and distilling self-supervised WavLM representations into a single codebook and optimizing an autoregressive next-chunk prediction objective. WavSLM jointly models semantic and acoustic information within a single token stream without text supervision or text pretraining. Despite its simplicity, it achieves competitive performance on consistency benchmarks and speech generation while using fewer parameters, less training data, and supporting streaming inference. Demo samples are available at https://lucadellalib.github.io/wavslm-web/.

</details>


### [109] [GALACTIC: Global and Local Agnostic Counterfactuals for Time-series Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05318)
*Christos Fragkathoulas,Eleni Psaroudaki,Themis Palpanas,Evaggelia Pitoura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GALACTIC是首个统一局部和全局反事实可解释性的无监督时间序列聚类框架。局部层面通过聚类感知优化生成跨聚类边界的最小扰动；全局层面利用MDL目标提取过渡聚类的非冗余解释摘要。方法具有超模性，可实现(1-1/e)近似保证的贪婪选择。在UCR档案上验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 时间序列聚类是模式发现的基本工具；2. 现有可解释性方法（基于特征归因或元数据）无法识别跨聚类边界的过渡；3. 反事实解释(CEs)主要用于监督设置，缺乏无监督时间序列聚类的反事实可解释性研究；4. 需要缓解认知负担并增强可解释性。

Method: 1. 局部层面：提出聚类感知优化目标，生成尊重目标聚类分配的扰动；2. 全局层面：提出基于最小描述长度(MDL)的目标函数，用于提取过渡聚类的全局解释的非冗余摘要；3. 理论证明：证明MDL目标是超模的，可转化为单调子模函数集优化问题；4. 算法：采用贪婪选择算法，具有可证明的(1-1/e)近似保证。

Result: GALACTIC在UCR档案数据集上产生明显更稀疏的局部反事实解释和比适应本问题的最先进基线方法更简洁的全局摘要，提供了通过反事实解释聚类时间序列的首个统一方法。

Conclusion: GALACTIC是首个统一局部和全局反事实可解释性的无监督时间序列聚类框架。通过MDL目标函数实现全局解释的非冗余摘要提取，并具有(1-1/e)的近似保证。在UCR档案上的广泛实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Abstract: Time-series clustering is a fundamental tool for pattern discovery, yet existing explainability methods, primarily based on feature attribution or metadata, fail to identify the transitions that move an instance across cluster boundaries. While Counterfactual Explanations (CEs) identify the minimal temporal perturbations required to alter the prediction of a model, they have been mostly confined to supervised settings. This paper introduces GALACTIC, the first unified framework to bridge local and global counterfactual explainability for unsupervised time-series clustering. At instance level (local), GALACTIC generates perturbations via a cluster-aware optimization objective that respects the target and underlying cluster assignments. At cluster level (global), to mitigate cognitive load and enhance interpretability, we formulate a representative CE selection problem. We propose a Minimum Description Length (MDL) objective to extract a non-redundant summary of global explanations that characterize the transitions between clusters. We prove that our MDL objective is supermodular, which allows the corresponding MDL reduction to be framed as a monotone submodular set function. This enables an efficient greedy selection algorithm with provable $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantees. Extensive experimental evaluation on the UCR Archive demonstrates that GALACTIC produces significantly sparser local CEs and more concise global summaries than state-of-the-art baselines adapted for our problem, offering the first unified approach for interpreting clustered time-series through counterfactuals.

</details>


### [110] [FairFinGAN: Fairness-aware Synthetic Financial Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05327)
*Tai Le Quy,Dung Nguyen Tuan,Trung Nguyen Thanh,Duy Tran Cong,Huyen Giang Thi Thu,Frank Hopfgartner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairFinGAN，一个基于WGAN的框架，用于生成合成金融数据的同时减轻对受保护属性的偏见。该方法通过在训练过程中直接集成公平性约束，确保合成数据既公平又能保持下游预测任务的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融数据集经常存在偏见，可能导致自动化系统中的不公平决策。现有的GAN数据生成方法缺乏有效的公平性保障机制，需要一种能够在生成合成数据的同时减轻偏见的解决方案。

Method: FairFinGAN采用WGAN架构，在训练过程中通过分类器直接嵌入公平性约束，确保合成数据既满足公平性要求，又能保留用于下游预测任务的实用性。

Result: 在五个真实金融数据集上的实验表明，该方法在实现优越的公平性指标的同时，未出现显著的效用损失，优于现有的基于GAN的数据生成方法。

Conclusion: FairFinGAN作为一种偏见感知的数据生成工具，在金融应用中具有显著潜力，能够有效平衡公平性与数据效用。

Abstract: Financial datasets often suffer from bias that can lead to unfair decision-making in automated systems. In this work, we propose FairFinGAN, a WGAN-based framework designed to generate synthetic financial data while mitigating bias with respect to the protected attribute. Our approach incorporates fairness constraints directly into the training process through a classifier, ensuring that the synthetic data is both fair and preserves utility for downstream predictive tasks. We evaluate our proposed model on five real-world financial datasets and compare it with existing GAN-based data generation methods. Experimental results show that our approach achieves superior fairness metrics without significant loss in data utility, demonstrating its potential as a tool for bias-aware data generation in financial applications.

</details>


### [111] [InfoFlow KV: Information-Flow-Aware KV Recomputation for Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05353)
*Xin Teng,Canyu Zhang,Shaoyi Zheng,Danyang Zhuo,Tianyi Zhou,Shengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息流引导的KV缓存选择性重计算方法，用于加速长上下文问答中的检索增强生成。通过利用查询的注意力范数信号识别语义相关且结构位置合适的token，并在推理一致的RoPE几何下计算，从而实现更高效的选择性KV重计算和chunk重排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文问答中，检索增强生成(RAG)在推理时需要对大量检索到的上下文进行预填充，这成为了计算瓶颈。现有方法依赖于启发式或表示差异来选择需要重计算的token，但未能建模所选token是否能有效影响生成结果。

Method: 将选择性KV重计算问题建模为信息流问题，提出使用查询的注意力范数信号来识别语义相关且结构位置上能传播信息的token。在推理一致的RoPE几何下计算该信号，重构检索chunk的全局位置分配，并引入信息流引导的chunk重排序策略。

Result: 在LLM和VLM基准测试上的实验表明，在相同的效率预算下，该方法相比先前方法获得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将选择性KV重计算问题转化为信息流问题，并利用推理一致的RoPE几何下的注意力范数信号，可以有效识别对生成有实际影响的关键token，从而在保持推理效率的同时提升长上下文问答的性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for long-context question answering is bottlenecked by inference-time prefilling over large retrieved contexts. A common strategy is to precompute key-value (KV) caches for individual documents and selectively recompute a small subset of tokens to restore global causal dependencies, but existing methods rely on heuristics or representation discrepancies without modeling whether selected tokens can effectively influence generation. We cast selective KV recomputation as an information flow problem and show that a simple attention-norm signal from the query reliably identifies tokens that are both semantically relevant and structurally positioned to propagate information, when computed under an inference-consistent RoPE geometry. We therefore reconstruct global positional assignments for retrieved chunks and introduce an information-flow-guided chunk reordering strategy. Experiments on LLM and VLM benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over prior methods under comparable efficiency budgets.

</details>


### [112] [Embedded Inter-Subject Variability in Adversarial Learning for Inertial Sensor-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05371)
*Francisco M. Calatrava-Nicolás,Shoko Miyauchi,Vitor Fortes Rey,Paul Lukowicz,Todor Stoyanov,Oscar Martinez Mozos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对可穿戴惯性传感器数据的人体活动识别(HAR)中主体间变异性导致的模型泛化问题，提出了一种新颖的深度对抗框架，通过在对抗任务中整合主体间变异性来促进主体不变特征表示的学习，在三个公开数据集上采用留一主体交叉验证进行评估，结果表明该方法有效减少了特征空间中的主体间差异，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体活动识别(HAR)面临的一个重要挑战是模型对未见过的新主体的泛化能力 due to inter-subject variability（主体间变异性），即不同个体执行同一活动的方式存在差异。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的深度对抗框架，将主体间变异性概念集成到对抗任务中。具体采用leave-one-subject-out (LOSO)交叉验证策略，在三个公开HAR数据集上进行评估。

Result: 该方法在三个公开HAR数据集上优于现有方法。进一步结果表明，所提出的对抗任务有效减少了不同用户在特征空间中的主体间变异性，并且在集成到框架中时优于以往工作中的对抗任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度对抗框架通过整合主体间变异性到对抗任务中，有效促进了主体不变特征表示的学习，显著提升了HAR模型对新主体的泛化能力。该方法在三个公开数据集上均优于现有方法，验证了其在减少特征空间中主体间变异方面的有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using data from wearable inertial sensors. An important challenge in HAR is the model's generalization capabilities to new unseen individuals due to inter-subject variability, i.e., the same activity is performed differently by different individuals. To address this problem, we propose a novel deep adversarial framework that integrates the concept of inter-subject variability in the adversarial task, thereby encouraging subject-invariant feature representations and enhancing the classification performance in the HAR problem. Our approach outperforms previous methods in three well-established HAR datasets using a leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation. Further results indicate that our proposed adversarial task effectively reduces inter-subject variability among different users in the feature space, and it outperforms adversarial tasks from previous works when integrated into our framework. Code: https://github.com/FranciscoCalatrava/EmbeddedSubjectVariability.git

</details>


### [113] [On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05433)
*Hejian Sang,Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Zhipeng Wang,Jiachen Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPSDC方法，通过自蒸馏让推理模型学会更简洁地思考，在Qwen3-8B/14B上实现57-59%token削减且准确率提升9-16分


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型产生的推理过程中大量内容是冗余的甚至是有害的，会累积错误，但现有方法缺乏有效压缩手段

Method: 核心方法是在"be concise"指令下获取教师logits，然后对学生自己的rollouts进行每token逆向KL散度最小化。无需真实答案、token预算或难度估计器

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-14B上，MATH-500任务实现57-59%token削减，准确率提升9-16分；AIME 2024上14B模型提升10分，压缩率达41%

Conclusion: OPSDC通过自蒸馏成功实现了推理压缩，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时大幅减少token使用，且模型能自动对简单问题进行激进压缩，对复杂问题保留必要推理

Abstract: Reasoning models think out loud, but much of what they say is noise. We introduce OPSDC (On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression), a method that teaches models to reason more concisely by
  distilling their own concise behavior back into themselves. The entire approach reduces to one idea: condition the same model on a "be concise" instruction to obtain teacher logits, and minimize per-token
  reverse KL on the student's own rollouts. No ground-truth answers, no token budgets, no difficulty estimators. Just self-distillation. Yet this simplicity belies surprising sophistication: OPSDC automatically
  compresses easy problems aggressively while preserving the deliberation needed for hard ones. On Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, we achieve 57-59% token reduction on MATH-500 while improving accuracy by 9-16 points
  absolute. On AIME 2024, the 14B model gains 10 points with 41% compression. The secret? Much of what reasoning models produce is not just redundant-it is actively harmful, compounding errors with every
  unnecessary token.

</details>


### [114] [Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05440)
*Siqi Yang,Kai Yan,Alexander G. Schwing,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的对抗性模仿学习方法LWAIL，通过在动态感知的潜在空间中计算Wasserstein距离来实现仅需少量状态-only专家演示即可达到专家水平性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习方法需要大量中高质量的演示以及专家动作信息，而这些在实际应用中往往难以获得。现有的方法在处理状态-only演示时效果有限。

Method: 提出LWAIL框架，核心创新包括：1) 采用状态-only分布匹配而非动作匹配；2) 在动态感知潜在空间中计算Wasserstein距离；3) 通过预训练ICVF (意图条件价值函数) 来捕获状态空间的动态结构，仅使用少量随机生成的状态数据即可完成预训练。

Result: 在多个MuJoCo环境中实验表明，该方法优于现有的基于Wasserstein的IL方法和对抗性IL方法，在各种任务中取得了更好的结果。只需一个或少量状态-only专家episode即可达到专家级性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入动态感知潜在空间和Wasserstein距离度量，成功实现了仅需极少量状态-only专家演示的高效模仿学习，为数据稀缺的IL应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation Learning (IL) enables agents to mimic expert behavior by learning from demonstrations. However, traditional IL methods require large amounts of medium-to-high-quality demonstrations as well as actions of expert demonstrations, both of which are often unavailable. To reduce this need, we propose Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning (LWAIL), a novel adversarial imitation learning framework that focuses on state-only distribution matching. It benefits from the Wasserstein distance computed in a dynamics-aware latent space. This dynamics-aware latent space differs from prior work and is obtained via a pre-training stage, where we train the Intention Conditioned Value Function (ICVF) to capture a dynamics-aware structure of the state space using a small set of randomly generated state-only data. We show that this enhances the policy's understanding of state transitions, enabling the learning process to use only one or a few state-only expert episodes to achieve expert-level performance. Through experiments on multiple MuJoCo environments, we demonstrate that our method outperforms prior Wasserstein-based IL methods and prior adversarial IL methods, achieving better results across various tasks.

</details>


### [115] [SurvHTE-Bench: A Benchmark for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation in Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05483)
*Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Xiaobin Shen,Jeremy C. Weiss,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurvHTE-Bench是首个用于右截尾生存数据异质性治疗效果估计的综合基准测试框架，包含合成、半合成和真实世界数据集，系统评估了多种因果生存方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从右截尾生存数据中估计异质性治疗效果在精准医疗和个体化决策等高风险应用中至关重要，但现有方法评估实践碎片化且不一致，缺乏统一的基准测试框架。

Method: 建立了包含三类数据集的基准测试框架：(1)具有已知真实值的模块化合成数据集，系统性变化因果假设和生存动态；(2)将真实世界协变量与模拟治疗和结果配对的半合成数据集；(3)来自双胞胎研究（已知真实值）和HIV临床试验的真实世界数据集。对多种生存HTE方法进行了系统比较。

Result: 提供了在合成、半合成和真实世界设置下生存HTE方法的首次严格比较，涵盖不同条件和现实假设违反情况。

Conclusion: SurvHTE-Bench为生存数据中HTE估计提供了首个全面、可重复和可扩展的评估框架，涵盖合成、半合成和真实世界数据集，并建立了公平比较的基础设施。

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects (HTEs) from right-censored survival data is critical in high-stakes applications such as precision medicine and individualized policy-making. Yet, the survival analysis setting poses unique challenges for HTE estimation due to censoring, unobserved counterfactuals, and complex identification assumptions. Despite recent advances, from Causal Survival Forests to survival meta-learners and outcome imputation approaches, evaluation practices remain fragmented and inconsistent. We introduce SurvHTE-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for HTE estimation with censored outcomes. The benchmark spans (i) a modular suite of synthetic datasets with known ground truth, systematically varying causal assumptions and survival dynamics, (ii) semi-synthetic datasets that pair real-world covariates with simulated treatments and outcomes, and (iii) real-world datasets from a twin study (with known ground truth) and from an HIV clinical trial. Across synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real-world settings, we provide the first rigorous comparison of survival HTE methods under diverse conditions and realistic assumption violations. SurvHTE-Bench establishes a foundation for fair, reproducible, and extensible evaluation of causal survival methods. The data and code of our benchmark are available at: https://github.com/Shahriarnz14/SurvHTE-Bench .

</details>


### [116] [POET-X: Memory-efficient LLM Training by Scaling Orthogonal Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05500)
*Zeju Qiu,Lixin Liu,Adrian Weller,Han Shi,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POET-X是一个可扩展且内存高效的正交等价变换训练框架，用于稳定高效地训练大规模语言模型，在单块Nvidia H100 GPU上即可完成十亿参数LLM的预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)的训练稳定性和效率仍是核心挑战。原有POET框架虽然提供强稳定性，但实现中存在高内存消耗和高计算开销的问题，限制了其在实际应用中的可扩展性。

Method: 引入POET-X，通过大幅降低计算成本的正交等价变换来实现可扩展和内存高效的训练。保持POET的正交等价变换核心思想，但优化了实现方式以减少矩阵乘法的计算负担。

Result: POET-X在单块Nvidia H100 GPU上成功完成十亿参数LLM的预训练，而标准AdamW优化器在相同设置下显存不足。POET-X在保持POET泛化性和稳定性的同时，实现了显著的吞吐量和内存效率提升。

Conclusion: POET-X成功解决了POET高内存消耗和计算开销的局限性，实现了在单GPU上训练十亿参数规模LLM的能力，为大规模模型的高效稳定训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Efficient and stable training of large language models (LLMs) remains a core challenge in modern machine learning systems. To address this challenge, Reparameterized Orthogonal Equivalence Training (POET), a spectrum-preserving framework that optimizes each weight matrix through orthogonal equivalence transformation, has been proposed. Although POET provides strong training stability, its original implementation incurs high memory consumption and computational overhead due to intensive matrix multiplications. To overcome these limitations, we introduce POET-X, a scalable and memory-efficient variant that performs orthogonal equivalence transformations with significantly reduced computational cost. POET-X maintains the generalization and stability benefits of POET while achieving substantial improvements in throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, POET-X enables the pretraining of billion-parameter LLMs on a single Nvidia H100 GPU, and in contrast, standard optimizers such as AdamW run out of memory under the same settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [iScript: A Domain-Adapted Large Language Model and Benchmark for Physical Design Tcl Script Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04476)
*Ning Xu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Senlin Shu,Lei Qi,Jiaqi Lv,Wensuo Wang,Tianhao Zhao,Chao Zhang,Zhaoliang Yang,Xiangyu Li,Zhaorui Su,Jingshan Li,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出iScript，一个针对Innovus Tcl脚本生成的领域适配Qwen3-8B模型，以及iScript-Bench基准测试。通过多阶段数据合成管道解决训练数据稀缺问题，采用两阶段训练策略和两步验证框架，实验表明领域适配和数据合成方法对EDA脚本任务有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM在EDA领域表现不佳，原因包括：极端的数据稀缺、领域特定语义以及物理设计所需的高可靠性。

Method: 1) 多阶段数据合成管道：命令提取、静态linting、需求反向推理和思维链生成，构建10K三元组数据集；2) 两阶段训练策略：领域自适应预训练+监督微调；3) 两步验证框架：静态语法验证+基于LLM的功能评估。

Result: iScript在iScript-Bench基准测试中平均pass@k分数高于当前最先进的LLM。

Conclusion: 研究证明了领域适应和数据合成对EDA脚本任务的有效性，iScript在基准测试中展现了比当前最先进的LLM更高的pass@k分数。

Abstract: Modern EDA flows rely heavily on Tcl scripting, yet general LLMs perform poorly in this domain due to extreme data scarcity, domain-specific semantics, and the high reliability required in physical design. We present iScript, a domain-adapted Qwen3-8B model for Innovus Tcl script generation, and iScript-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark covering five task categories and three difficulty levels. To overcome the lack of training data, we introduce a multi-stage data synthesis pipeline that integrates command extraction, static linting, requirement back-inference, and Chain-of-Thought generation, producing a 10K-tuple (requirement, CoT, script) dataset. iScript is trained through a two-stage strategy combining domain-adaptive pretraining and supervised fine-tuning. To evaluate script correctness efficiently, we further propose a two-step verification framework consisting of static syntax verification and LLM-based functional evaluation. On our benchmark, iScript shows higher pass@k scores than currently state-of-the-art LLMs on average. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of domain adaptation and data synthesis for EDA scripting tasks.

</details>


### [118] [CLARC: C/C++ Benchmark for Robust Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04484)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Weike Fang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CLARC，一个C/C++代码检索基准测试，通过自动化pipeline和挑战性设置（标识符匿名化、低级语言编译）测试模型鲁棒性，发现现有SOTA模型仍过度依赖词汇匹配而非语义理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码检索基准测试主要关注Python语言，缺乏对代码语义理解的深度测试，且很少检验模型在超出表面词汇线索情况下的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了一个自动化pipeline用于代码搜索数据集生成，包含代码可编译性保障、依赖复杂度分类（区分自定义类型或辅助函数依赖），并引入标识符匿名化、编译为汇编和WebAssembly等挑战性设置。创建了CLARC基准测试，包含1,245个评估对和5,472个训练对，使用LLM生成自然语言查询并通过人工评分和假设验证进行严格筛选。

Result: 对6个最先进的代码检索模型进行评估，发现在挑战性条件下检索效果显著下降。

Conclusion: 当前模型持续依赖词汇特征而非代码语义理解，强调需要开发更具语义理解能力的代码检索方法。

Abstract: Efficient code retrieval is critical for developer productivity, yet existing benchmarks largely focus on Python and rarely stress-test robustness beyond superficial lexical cues. To address the gap, we introduce an automated pipeline for code search datasets and present CLARC, a C/C++ benchmark built from real-world GitHub repositories. CLARC contains 1,245 query-code pairs for evaluation and 5,472 pairs for training. The benchmark incorporates LLM-generated natural language queries validated through rigorous human scoring and hypothesis testing. To analyze contextual requirements effectively, our pipeline starts by ensuring code compilability. It then categorizes code snippets by dependency complexity, distinguishing whether the code relies on custom-defined types or helper functions. The pipeline also enables CLARC to stress-test retrieval robustness by introducing challenging settings, including identifier anonymization and compilation to low-level languages like Assembly and WebAssembly. Under these conditions, our evaluation of six state-of-the-art models reveals sharp drops in retrieval effectiveness. The experimental results highlight the models' persistent reliance on lexical features rather than code semantic understanding. Our dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ClarcTeam/CLARC.

</details>


### [119] [Industrial Survey on Robustness Testing In Cyber Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04587)
*Christophe Ponsard,Abiola Paterne Chokki,Jean-François Daune*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对瓦隆尼亚地区多个行业的工业调查，评估CPS鲁棒性的当前实践状态，涵盖需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、故障模式和工具等方面，识别出行业实践与最先进方法之间的关键挑战和差距，并与文献中的类似调查进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS在现代工业中扮演关键角色（制造、能源、交通、医疗），其故障可能导致重大经济、运营和安全后果，因此确保这些系统的鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 在瓦隆尼亚地区开展了涵盖多个行业的工业调查，评估CPS鲁棒性的当前实践状态，调查涵盖需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、故障模式及可用工具等方面，并将结果与文献中的类似工业调查进行对比。

Result: 识别出CPS鲁棒性面临的关键挑战和差距，包括行业实践与学术界最优方法之间的不一致之处。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了工业实践与当前最优方法之间存在显著差距，为CPS鲁棒性研究提供了实证基础，并为未来改进工业实践和学术研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) play a critical role in modern industrial domains, including manufacturing, energy, transportation, and healthcare, where they enable automation, optimization, and real-time decision-making. Ensuring the robustness of these systems is paramount, as failures can have significant economic, operational, and safety consequences. This paper present findings from an industrial survey conducted in Wallonia, covering a wide range of sectors, to assess the current state of practice in CPS robustness. It investigates robustness from how it is understood and applied in relationship with requirements engineering, system design, test execution, failure modes, and available tools. It identifies key challenges and gaps between industry practices and state-of-the-art methodologies. Additionally, it compares our findings with similar industrial surveys from the literature.

</details>


### [120] [Behaviour Driven Development Scenario Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04729)
*Amila Rathnayake,Mojtaba Shahin,Golnoush Abaei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了GPT-4、Claude 3和Gemini三种大语言模型在自动生成行为驱动开发(BDD)场景方面的表现。通过构建包含500个用户故事、需求描述及其对应BDD场景的数据集，采用文本/语义相似度指标、LLM评估和人类专家评估的多维框架进行评价。研究发现GPT-4在相似度指标上得分最高，但Claude 3生成的场景在人类和LLM评估中评分最高，且DeepSeek等LLM评估器与人类判断的相关性高于传统相似度指标。提示技术的有效性因模型而异，输入质量对生成效果有决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着敏捷开发的普及，BDD已成为连接需求与测试的关键实践。然而，手动编写BDD场景耗时且易出错。现有研究主要关注LLM在代码生成或测试生成方面的应用，缺乏针对BDD场景生成的系统评估。因此，有必要对主流LLM在自动化BDD场景生成任务中的表现进行全面评估，以指导实践中的模型选择和提示策略优化。

Method: 研究构建了包含500个样本的数据集，来源于四个专有软件产品的用户故事、需求描述及其对应BDD场景。采用多维评估框架：(1)文本相似度指标(BLEU、ROUGE等)；(2)语义相似度指标(基于嵌入的相似度)；(3)LLM评估器(如DeepSeek)自动评分；(4)人类专家评分。同时针对不同模型测试了零样本提示、思维链推理和少样本提示三种技术，并对比了温度和top_p参数的影响。

Result: 主要发现包括：1)GPT-4在文本和语义相似度指标上得分最高；2)Claude 3生成的场景在人类专家和LLM评估中均获得最高评分；3)DeepSeek等LLM评估器与人类判断的相关性强于传统相似度指标；4)提示技术效果具有模型特异性：GPT-4适合零样本，Claude 3受益于思维链推理，Gemini在少样本提示下表现最佳；5)输入质量决定生成效果：详细需求描述可生成高质量场景，单独使用用户故事则效果较差；6)最优参数配置为temperature=0、top_p=1.0。

Conclusion: 不同LLM在不同评估维度上各有优势，模型选择应基于具体评估标准。LLM作为评估器的有效性值得认可，其与人类判断的高度相关性表明可作为有效的自动化评估工具。提示工程需要针对特定模型进行优化，输入质量是关键因素。temperature=0和top_p=1.0是生成高质量BDD场景的最佳配置。未来研究可扩展评估更多LLM并探索更精细的参数调优策略。

Abstract: This paper presents an evaluation of three LLMs, GPT-4, Claude 3, and Gemini, for automated Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) scenarios generation. To support this evaluation, we constructed a dataset of 500 user stories, requirement descriptions, and their corresponding BDD scenarios, drawn from four proprietary software products. We assessed the quality of BDD scenarios generated by LLMs using a multidimensional evaluation framework encompassing text and semantic similarity metrics, LLM-based evaluation, and human expert assessment. Our findings reveal that although GPT-4 achieves higher scores in text and semantic similarity metrics, Claude 3 produces scenarios rated highest by both human experts and LLM-based evaluators. LLM-based evaluators, particularly DeepSeek, show a stronger correlation with human judgment than with text similarity and semantic similarity metrics. The effectiveness of prompting techniques is model-specific: GPT-4 performs best with zero-shot, Claude 3 benefits from chain-of-thought reasoning, and Gemini achieves optimal results with few-shot examples. Input quality determines the effectiveness of BDD scenario generation: detailed requirement descriptions alone yield high-quality scenarios, whereas user stories alone yield low-quality scenarios. Our experiments indicate that setting temperature to 0 and top_p to 1.0 produced the highest-quality BDD scenarios across all models.

</details>


### [121] [Public Sector Open Source Program Offices - Archetypes for how to Grow (Common) Institutional Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04891)
*Johan Linåker,Astor Nummelin Carlberg,Ciaran O'Riordan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了欧洲公共部门组织中开源项目办公室(OSPO)如何促进开源软件的采用、发展与协作。通过对欧盟、挪威、列支敦士登和冰岛16个公共部门OSPO案例的访谈调查，研究识别出六种OSPO原型，并为公共部门组织设计和实施OSPO提供了基于实证的政策建议和实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件(OSS)是支撑工业和公共数字服务的基础数字基础设施的关键组成部分，在欧洲公共部门的重要性通过多项部长级声明得到强调，突出了其加速数字化转型、促进商业发展和培养数字技能人口的潜力。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对来自16个公共部门OSPO案例的18名OSPO代表进行访谈调查，并将这些案例交叉分析和归纳为六种OSPO原型。研究结果通过两场后续焦点小组讨论（包括早期受访者和额外专家）进行了验证和丰富。

Result: 研究识别出六种不同的OSPO原型，提供了关于其组织结构、职责和OSS采用贡献的见解。这些原型与政策建议一起，为PSO如何根据其特定背景、资源和政策目标设计自己的OSPO提供了指导。

Conclusion: 研究结果增强了对OSPO作为促进OSS采用的战略举措的理解，为公共 sector组织(PSO)和政策制定者提供了实践指导，帮助他们利用OSS实现战略目标、促进数字主权、推动经济增长并提高数字服务的互操作性和质量。

Abstract: Context: Open Source Software (OSS) is a crucial component of over 90\% of digital infrastructure underpinning industry and public digital services, facilitating collaborative software development and dissemination. Its significance in the European public sector has been emphasised through various Ministerial Declarations, highlighting its potential to accelerate digitalisation, transform businesses, and foster a digitally skilled population. Research Aim: This study aims to explore how the adoption, development, and collaboration on OSS can be enabled through organisational support functions or centres of competency, also known as Open Source Programme Offices (OSPOs) within Public Sector Organisations (PSOs) in the European Union, Norway, Liechtenstein, and Iceland. Methodology: A qualitative research approach was adopted, involving an interview survey of 18 OSPO representatives across 16 cases of public-sector OSPOs. These cases were cross-analysed and categorised into six OSPO archetypes. The findings were validated and enriched through two follow-up focus groups that included earlier interviewees and additional experts. Results: The study identified six distinct OSPO archetypes, providing insights into their organisational structures, responsibilities, and contributions to OSS adoption. The archetypes, along with policy recommendations, offer guidance on how PSOs can design their own OSPOs, taking into account their specific context, resources, and policy goals. Conclusions: The findings enhance the understanding of OSPOs as strategic endeavours aimed at promoting OSS adoption. The study offers practical guidance for PSOs and policymakers on leveraging OSS to achieve strategic objectives, foster digital sovereignty, drive economic growth, and improve the interoperability and quality of digital services.

</details>


### [122] [RepoLaunch: Automating Build&Test Pipeline of Code Repositories on ANY Language and ANY Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05026)
*Kenan Li,Rongzhi Li,Linghao Zhang,Qirui Jin,Liao Zhu,Xiaosong Huang,Geng Zhang,Yikai Zhang,Shilin He,Chengxing Xie,Xin Zhang,Zijian Jin,Bowen Li,Chaoyun Zhang,Yu Kang,Yufan Huang,Elsie Nallipogu,Saravan Rajmohan,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoLaunch是首个能够自动解析依赖关系、编译源代码并提取测试结果的智能体，支持任意编程语言和操作系统。提出首个完全自动化的SWE数据集创建流水线，仅需人工干预任务设计环节，已被多个 agentic 基准测试和训练工作采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建软件仓库通常需要大量手动 effort，近期 LLM agents 在软件工程中的自动化进展加速了这一领域的发展，但此前缺乏能够跨语言、跨操作系统自动完成仓库构建的通用工具。

Method: 设计并实现 RepoLaunch agent，具备自动解析依赖、编译源代码、提取测试结果的能力；基于此提出完全自动化的 SWE 数据集创建流水线，仅任务设计需人工介入。

Result: RepoLaunch 已被多个 agentic 基准测试和训练工作采用，实现了可扩展的编码 agents 和 LLMs 基准测试与训练数据生成。

Conclusion: RepoLaunch 实现了软件仓库构建的全面自动化，显著降低了 SWE 数据集创建的人力成本，为编码 agents 和 LLMs 的可扩展基准测试与训练奠定了基础。

Abstract: Building software repositories typically requires significant manual effort. Recent advances in large language model (LLM) agents have accelerated automation in software engineering (SWE). We introduce RepoLaunch, the first agent capable of automatically resolving dependencies, compiling source code, and extracting test results for repositories across arbitrary programming languages and operating systems. To demonstrate its utility, we further propose a fully automated pipeline for SWE dataset creation, where task design is the only human intervention. RepoLaunch automates the remaining steps, enabling scalable benchmarking and training of coding agents and LLMs. Notably, several works on agentic benchmarking and training have recently adopted RepoLaunch for automated task generation.

</details>


### [123] [Why Do You Contribute to Stack Overflow? Understanding Cross-Cultural Motivations and Usage Patterns before the Age of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05043)
*Sherlock A. Licorish,Elijah Zolduoarrati,Tony Savarimuthu,Rashina Hoda,Ronnie De Souza Santos,Pankajeshwara Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了Stack Overflow贡献者的动机差异，分析美国、中国和俄罗斯三个国家的区域差异及其与平台活动的关系。通过定性内容分析和定量语言分析，识别出17种动机类别，发现广告机会和利他性问题解决是主要动机，并揭示了不同文化背景用户在自我展示和学习导向方面的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解社区问答平台贡献者的参与动机对于维持知识共享生态系统至关重要，尤其是在LLM时代，这些平台数据被用于模型训练。然而，现有关于贡献者动机的研究缺乏跨文化视角，特别是不同国家文化背景下动机如何变化的研究有限。

Method: 采用混合研究方法：1）定性内容分析Stack Overflow用户档案；2）定量语言分析美国、中国和俄罗斯的用户数据；3）使用演绎内容分析识别动机类别；4）应用相关分析探索动机与平台活动之间的关联。

Result: 识别出17种动机类别。贡献者主要受广告机会和利他性问题解决欲望驱动。美国贡献者表现出更强的自我推广行为，中国贡献者展现出更强的学习导向参与。相关性分析显示，档案更详细的用户更倾向于广告和社交活动，而学习导向用户保持较低程度的自我展示。

Conclusion: 不同文化背景下Stack Overflow贡献者的动机存在显著差异。理解这些文化差异可为制定增强软件工程领域跨文化参与的策略提供参考，帮助平台运营者针对不同地区用户设计差异化的激励机制。

Abstract: Understanding motivations of contributors for participating in community question and answer platforms is crucial for sustaining knowledge-sharing ecosystem, which is necessary to advance the discipline while also ensuring its longevity. This is particularly necessary in the age of LLMs, where data from such portals are used to train these models. Limited insights exist regarding how motivations of contributors vary across different national cultures. This research investigates Stack Overflow contributor motivations, analysing regional differences and relations to platform activity. We combined qualitative content analysis of Stack Overflow profiles with quantitative linguistic analysis of data from the United States, China, and Russia. Using deductive content analysis, we identified 17 motivational categories. We applied correlation analysis to identify associations between stated motivations and platform activities. Contributors are primarily motivated by advertising opportunities and altruistic problem solving desires. American contributors demonstrated stronger self promotional behaviours while Chinese contributors exhibited greater learning oriented engagement. Our correlation analysis showed that those with more detailed profiles tend to engage in advertising and social activities, while learning oriented users maintain minimal self presentation. Understanding these variations can inform strategies for enhancing cross cultural participation in software engineering.

</details>


### [124] [A Benchmarking Framework for Model Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05250)
*Philipp-Lorenz Glaser,Lola Burgueño,Dominik Bork*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向模型驱动工程(MDE)软件模型数据集的基准测试框架，旨在系统性地评估和比较数据集的质量、代表性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型驱动工程中的实证研究和基于大语言模型的研究越来越依赖软件模型数据集，但这些数据集通常是临时收集的，缺乏质量保证和任务适用性验证。这导致研究结果难以比较、数据集质量和代表性不明确、实验可重复性差以及潜在的偏差问题。

Method: 提出了一个MDE基准测试平台(Benchmark Platform for MDE)，提供统一的基础设施，使用定义的指标和标准，系统性地评估和比较不同语言和格式的软件模型数据集的质量、代表性和特定任务的适用性。

Result: 该框架为数据集评估提供了系统化的方法，使研究者能够更科学地选择和使用数据集，提高研究的可比性和可重复性。

Conclusion: 将数据集视为一等公民进行基准测试是提升模型驱动工程研究质量的重要步骤，该平台有望改善该领域数据集管理的实践。

Abstract: Empirical and LLM-based research in model-driven engineering increasingly relies on datasets of software models, for instance, to train or evaluate machine learning techniques for modeling support. These datasets have a significant impact on solution performance; hence, they should be treated and assessed as first-class artifacts. However, such datasets are typically collected or created ad hoc and without guarantees of their quality for the specific task for which they are used. This limits the comparability of results between studies, obscures dataset quality and representativeness, and leads to weak reproducibility and potential bias. In this work, we propose a benchmarking framework for model datasets (i.e., benchmarking the dataset itself). Benchmarking datasets involves systematically measuring their quality, representativeness, and suitability for specific tasks. To this end, we propose a Benchmark Platform for MDE that provides a unified infrastructure for systematically assessing and comparing datasets of software models across languages and formats, using defined criteria and metrics.

</details>


### [125] [A framework for assessing the capabilities of code generation of constraint domain-specific languages with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05278)
*David Delgado,Lola Burgueño,Robert Clarisó*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，用于评估大语言模型(LLM)从文本规范生成领域特定语言(DSL)代码的能力，并以约束语言(OCL和Alloy)为实例，与Python进行对比实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程任务中表现出色，但其有效性在处理不太流行的编程语言（如DSL）时显著下降，缺乏系统性的评估方法来衡量LLM生成DSL代码的能力。

Method: 提出一个通用评估框架，从代码的良形性(well-formedness)和正确性(correctness)两个角度评估生成的代码，并以约束语言(OCL和Alloy)为案例进行实验，同时与Python对比。

Result: 实验表明：1)LLM对Python的表现优于OCL和 Alloy；2)小型上下文窗口的开源LLM无法生成约束相关代码；3)代码修复和多轮尝试可提升生成质量；4)提示模板选择影响较小。

Conclusion: 该评估框架可系统分析代码生成的各个维度，帮助确定特定任务的最优代码生成配置。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be used to support software development tasks, e.g., through code completion or code generation. However, their effectiveness drops significantly when considering less popular programming languages such as domain-specific languages (DSLs). In this paper, we propose a generic framework for evaluating the capabilities of LLMs generating DSL code from textual specifications. The generated code is assessed from the perspectives of well-formedness and correctness. This framework is applied to a particular type of DSL, constraint languages, focusing our experiments on OCL and Alloy and comparing their results to those achieved for Python, a popular general-purpose programming language. Experimental results show that, in general, LLMs have better performance for Python than for OCL and Alloy. LLMs with smaller context windows such as open-source LLMs may be unable to generate constraint-related code, as this requires managing both the constraint and the domain model where it is defined. Moreover, some improvements to the code generation process such as code repair (asking an LLM to fix incorrect code) or multiple attempts (generating several candidates for each coding task) can improve the quality of the generated code. Meanwhile, other decisions like the choice of a prompt template have less impact. All these dimensions can be systematically analyzed using our evaluation framework, making it possible to decide the most effective way to set up code generation for a particular type of task.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [From Spark to Fire: Modeling and Mitigating Error Cascades in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04474)
*Yizhe Xie,Congcong Zhu,Xinyue Zhang,Tianqing Zhu,Dayong Ye,Minfeng Qi,Huajie Chen,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型多智能体系统(LLM-MAS)中协作机制导致的错误放大问题，提出传播动态模型识别三类漏洞，并设计基于家谱图的治理层作为消息层插件防御，将防御成功率从0.32提升至0.89以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-MAS在复杂协作场景中应用广泛，但其协作机制可能导致微小的不准确通过迭代逐渐固化为系统级的虚假共识。错误通过消息依赖传播和放大，风险难以追踪。现有防护依赖单智能体验证或需要修改协作架构，可能削弱有效信息流且与真实任务中的自然协作过程不符。

Method: 提出针对LLM-MAS的传播动态模型，将协作抽象为有向依赖图，提供早期风险标准来表征放大风险。通过在六个主流框架上的实验，识别出三类漏洞：级联放大、拓扑敏感性和共识惯性。进一步实现了一种攻击，仅注入单个原子错误种子即可导致广泛失败。引入基于家谱图的治理层，作为消息层插件实现，无需改变协作架构即可抑制内生和外生错误放大。

Result: 实验表明，该方法将防御成功率从基线0.32提高到0.89以上，并显著减轻了微小错误的级联传播。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLM-MAS中协作机制的安全隐患，通过传播动态模型和家谱图治理层提供了有效的防御方案，在不改变协作架构的前提下实现了对错误放大的抑制。

Abstract: Large Language Model-based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly applied to complex collaborative scenarios. However, their collaborative mechanisms may cause minor inaccuracies to gradually solidify into system-level false consensus through iteration. Such risks are difficult to trace since errors can propagate and amplify through message dependencies. Existing protections often rely on single-agent validation or require modifications to the collaboration architecture, which can weaken effective information flow and may not align with natural collaboration processes in real tasks. To address this, we propose a propagation dynamics model tailored for LLM-MAS that abstracts collaboration as a directed dependency graph and provides an early-stage risk criterion to characterize amplification risk. Through experiments on six mainstream frameworks, we identify three vulnerability classes: cascade amplification, topological sensitivity, and consensus inertia. We further instantiate an attack where injecting just a single atomic error seed leads to widespread failure. In response, we introduce a genealogy-graph-based governance layer, implemented as a message-layer plugin, that suppresses both endogenous and exogenous error amplification without altering the collaboration architecture. Experiments show that this approach raises the defense success rate from a baseline of 0.32 to over 0.89 and significantly mitigates the cascading spread of minor errors.

</details>


### [127] [Strategic Interactions in Multi-Level Stackelberg Games with Non-Follower Agents and Heterogeneous Leaders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04628)
*Niloofar Aminikalibar,Farzaneh Farhadi,Maria Chli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出三层Stackelberg博弈框架，解决传统模型忽略非跟随者的问题，应用于EV充电基础设施，证明考虑非跟随者和异质性竞争者会改变均衡预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Stackelberg博弈模型忽略了不直接参与市场竞争但会适应拥堵的非跟随者 agents，导致拥堵耦合市场的均衡预测存在系统性偏差。

Method: 提出三层Stackelberg框架：异质性领导者（决策视野和可行行为不同）、战略跟随者、以及非跟随者 agents。应用于电动汽车充电基础设施场景。

Result: 模型表明显式考虑非跟随者和异质性竞争者会定性改变战略激励和均衡结果。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于移动、能源和计算市场中广泛的拥堵耦合多智能体系统。

Abstract: Strategic interaction in congested systems is commonly modelled using Stackelberg games, where competing leaders anticipate the behaviour of self-interested followers. A key limitation of existing models is that they typically ignore agents who do not directly participate in market competition, yet both contribute to and adapt to congestion. Although such non-follower agents do not generate revenue or respond to market incentives, their behaviour reshapes congestion patterns, which in turn affects the decisions of leaders and followers through shared resources.
  We argue that overlooking non-followers leads to systematically distorted equilibrium predictions in congestion-coupled markets. To address this, we introduce a three-level Stackelberg framework with heterogeneous leaders differing in decision horizons and feasible actions, strategic followers, and non-follower agents that captures bidirectional coupling between infrastructure decisions, competition, and equilibrium congestion.
  We instantiate the framework in the context of electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure, where charging providers compete with rivals, while EV and non-EV traffic jointly shape congestion. The model illustrates how explicitly accounting for non-followers and heterogeneous competitors qualitatively alters strategic incentives and equilibrium outcomes. Beyond EV charging, the framework applies to a broad class of congestion-coupled multi-agent systems in mobility, energy, and computing markets.

</details>


### [128] [SCoUT: Scalable Communication via Utility-Guided Temporal Grouping in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04833)
*Manav Vora,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: SCoUT通过时间抽象和智能体抽象解决部分可观测多智能体强化学习中通信的缩放问题和信用分配问题，使用Gumbel-Softmax采样软智能体组作为可微分先验，组感知Critic降低复杂度，通过反事实通信优势实现精确信用分配，保留去中心化执行特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 通信可以改善部分可观测MARL中的协调问题；2) 学习和谁通信需要在众多发送者-接收者对中做出选择；3) 单条信息对未来奖励的影响难以隔离，导致通信信用分配困难。

Method: 1) 使用Gumbel-Softmax每K个环境步骤重采样软智能体组，作为可微分的接收者先验；2) 组感知Critic预测每组价值并通过软分配映射到个体基线，降低Critic复杂度并减少方差；3) 三头策略：环境动作、发送决策、接收者选择；4) 通过反事实分析移除发送者贡献来计算通信优势，实现精确信用分配。

Result: 该方法在训练过程中通过软智能体组实现可扩展通信，通过反事实通信优势实现精确的发送和接收者选择信用分配，执行时丢弃所有集中式组件仅保留每智能体策略实现去中心化执行。

Conclusion: SCoUT通过时间抽象和智能体抽象解决了部分可观测多智能体强化学习中的通信缩放问题，通过反事实通信优势实现精确信用分配，训练时使用集中式 Critic 指导，执行时完全去中心化。实验效果需要在原论文中验证。

Abstract: Communication can improve coordination in partially observed multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), but learning \emph{when} and \emph{who} to communicate with requires choosing among many possible sender-recipient pairs, and the effect of any single message on future reward is hard to isolate. We introduce \textbf{SCoUT} (\textbf{S}calable \textbf{Co}mmunication via \textbf{U}tility-guided \textbf{T}emporal grouping), which addresses both these challenges via temporal and agent abstraction within traditional MARL. During training, SCoUT resamples \textit{soft} agent groups every \(K\) environment steps (macro-steps) via Gumbel-Softmax; these groups are latent clusters that induce an affinity used as a differentiable prior over recipients. Using the same assignments, a group-aware critic predicts values for each agent group and maps them to per-agent baselines through the same soft assignments, reducing critic complexity and variance. Each agent is trained with a three-headed policy: environment action, send decision, and recipient selection. To obtain precise communication learning signals, we derive counterfactual communication advantages by analytically removing each sender's contribution from the recipient's aggregated messages. This counterfactual computation enables precise credit assignment for both send and recipient-selection decisions. At execution time, all centralized training components are discarded and only the per-agent policy is run, preserving decentralized execution. Project website, videos and code: \hyperlink{https://scout-comm.github.io/}{https://scout-comm.github.io/}

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Overcoming Latency-bound Limitations of Distributed Graph Algorithms using the HPX Runtime System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04583)
*Karame Mohammadiporshokooh,Panagiotis Syskakis,Andrew Lumsdaine,Hartmut Kaiser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HPX高性能运行时的分布式图处理库原型，实现了BFS、PageRank和三角形计数三种图算法，在性能和执行效率上优于GraphX和PBGL等传统框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式图处理框架（如Spark GraphX和PBGL）存在负载不均衡和同步开销问题，难以有效处理大规模图计算。

Method: 使用NWgraph库的C++机制和HPX的分布式容器及异步构造，实现了三种代表性图算法（BFS遍历、PageRank中心性、三角形计数模式匹配），采用统一执行模型将本地和远程计算使用相同编程抽象表达，由运行时透明管理异步性。

Result: HPX实现通过利用异步执行、延迟隐藏和共享内存细粒度并行性，性能显著优于GraphX和PBGL。

Conclusion: 该设计明确利用每个节点内的共享内存并行性，同时重叠跨节点的通信与计算，为扩展到更广泛的分布式图算法提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Graph processing at scale presents many challenges, including the irregular structure of graphs, the latency-bound nature of graph algorithms, and the overhead associated with distributed execution. While existing frameworks such as Spark GraphX and the Parallel Boost Graph Library (PBGL) have introduced abstractions for distributed graph processing, they continue to struggle with inherent issues like load imbalance and synchronization overhead. In this work, we present a distributed library prototype and a distributed implementation of three key graph algorithms - Breadth-First Search (BFS), PageRank, and Triangle Counting, using C++ mechanisms from the NWgraph library and leveraging HPX's distributed containers and asynchronous constructs. These algorithms span the categories of Traversal, centrality, and Pattern matching, and are selected to represent diverse computational characteristics. We evaluate our HPX-based implementations against GraphX, and PBGL, showing that a high-performance runtime such as HPX enables the construction of algorithms that significantly outperform conventional frameworks by exploiting asynchronous execution, latency hiding, and fine-grained parallelism in shared memory. All algorithms in our prototype follow a unified execution model in which local and remote computations are expressed using the same programming abstractions, with asynchrony managed transparently by the runtime. This design explicitly leverages shared-memory parallelism within each locality while overlapping communication and computation across localities, providing a practical foundation for extending this approach to a broader class of distributed graph algorithms.

</details>


### [130] [DuaLip-GPU Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04621)
*Gregory Dexter,Aida Rahmattalabi,Sanjana Garg,Qinquan Song,Ruby Tu,Yuan Gao,Yi Zhang,Zhipeng Wang,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大规模线性规划的GPU加速求解器架构，通过算子中心编程模型实现问题规范与优化引擎的解耦，并开发了针对稀疏匹配约束的专用GPU执行技术，在极端规模匹配问题上实现10倍以上的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECLIPSE和LinkedIn的DuaLip求解器紧密耦合于少量模式，基于CPU中心的Scala/Spark堆栈，限制了可扩展性且无法有效利用现代加速器。

Method: 采用算子中心编程模型，使用可组合的原语表达对偶目标评估和分块投影算子；开发了约束对齐的稀疏布局、批处理投影内核和仅通信对偶变量的分布式设计；改进了岭正则化对偶上升方法，加入Jacobi风格行归一化、原始缩放和正则化参数的延续方案。

Result: 在极端规模匹配工作负载上，GPU实现相比分布式CPU DuaLip求解器在相同停止准则下实现了至少10倍的wall-clock加速，同时保持收敛保证。

Conclusion: 提出了一种解耦问题规范与优化引擎的GPU加速求解器架构，通过算子中心编程模型和针对稀疏匹配约束的GPU执行技术，在极端规模匹配工作负载上实现了至少10倍的加速，同时保持收敛保证。

Abstract: Large-scale linear programs (LPs) arise in many decision systems, including ranking, allocation, and matching problems that must be solved repeatedly at massive scale. Prior work such as ECLIPSE and LinkedIn's open-source DuaLip showed that ridge-regularized dual ascent with first-order methods can scale to these settings. However, the original implementation was tightly coupled to a small number of schemas and built on a CPU-centric Scala/Spark stack, limiting extensibility and preventing effective use of modern accelerators.
  We present a redesigned solver architecture that decouples problem specification from the optimization engine and targets GPU execution. The system uses an operator-centric programming model in which LP formulations are expressed through composable primitives for dual objective evaluation and blockwise projection operators for decomposable constraint families. This design allows new formulations to be added locally while reusing a shared optimization loop, diagnostics, and distributed infrastructure.
  To realize the available parallelism, we develop GPU execution techniques tailored to sparse matching constraints, including constraint-aligned sparse layouts, batched projection kernels, and a distributed design that communicates only dual variables. Further, we improve the underlying ridge-regularized dual ascent method with Jacobi-style row normalization, primal scaling, and a continuation scheme for the regularization parameter.
  On extreme-scale matching workloads, the GPU implementation achieves at least a 10x wall-clock speedup over the prior distributed CPU DuaLip solver under matched stopping criteria, while maintaining convergence guarantees.

</details>


### [131] [SLO-Aware Compute Resource Allocation for Prefill-Decode Disaggregated LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04716)
*Luchang Li,Dongfang Li,Bozhao Gong,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对LLM推理中预填充-解码（P/D）分离架构的硬件资源优化配置问题，提出一种结合理论建模与经验基准测试的混合方法，利用M/M/1排队理论确定预填充吞吐量，通过经验测量确定解码批量大小，实现最优P/D资源分配预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理中P/D（预填充/解码）分离已成为广泛采用的优化策略，但目前缺乏在总吞吐量、服务等级目标（SLO）和请求特征（输入/输出长度）约束下确定最优P/D硬件资源数量的成熟方法

Method: 提出一种结合理论建模与经验基准测试的混合方法：1）基于总吞吐量需求、请求输入输出长度以及预填充和解码吞吐量建立理论模型计算P/D资源数量；2）使用M/M/1排队理论建模预填充过程，从基准测试的最大预填充吞吐量和首Token生成时间（TTFT）推导实际预填充吞吐量；3）对于解码阶段，确定满足每输出Token时间（TPOT）要求的解码批量大小，并通过经验测量获得相应解码吞吐量

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法能够准确预测真实LLM推理场景中的最优P/D资源分配

Conclusion: 该混合方法可有效解决P/D硬件资源优化配置问题，通过理论模型与经验测试相结合的方式，在满足SLO约束条件下实现资源的最优分配

Abstract: Prefill-Decode (P/D) disaggregation has emerged as a widely adopted optimization strategy for Large Language Model (LLM) inference. However, there currently exists no well-established methodology for determining the optimal number of P/D hardware resources, subject to constraints on total throughput, service level objectives (SLOs), and request characteristics - specifically input and output lengths. To address this gap, we propose a hybrid approach that combines theoretical modeling with empirical benchmarking. First, we present a theoretical model for calculating P/D resource counts, which is based on total throughput requirements, request input and output lengths, as well as prefill and decode throughput. Then, to obtain the actual prefill and decode throughput under SLO constraints, we model the prefill process using M/M/1 queuing theory, deriving the achieved prefill throughput from the benchmarked maximum prefill throughput and Time-To-First-Token (TTFT). For the decode phase, we determine the decode batch sizes that meet Time-Per-Output-Token (TPOT) requirements and obtain the corresponding decode throughput through empirical measurements. Our experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method can accurately predict optimal P/D resource allocation in real-world LLM inference scenarios.

</details>


### [132] [The Semantic Arrow of Time, Part III: RDMA and the Completion Fallacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04774)
*Paul Borrill*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是"The Semantic Arrow of Time"系列的第三篇论文，探讨RDMA（远程直接内存访问）技术中的"完成谬误"——RDMA保证数据放置但不能保证应用语义上的承诺。论文通过四个案例研究和比较分析，论证只有具备强制反射阶段的协议架构才能消除放置与承诺之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meta、Google、Microsoft等公司大规模部署RDMA技术（Meta的24000-GPU集群、Google数据中心、Azure基础设施），但作者认为RDMA的完成语义存在根本性缺陷：保证数据写入远程NIC缓冲区（放置），但不保证数据被接收应用语义整合（承诺）。这一差距可能任意大，且现有解决方案均无法完全解决。

Method: 1）通过RDMA Write操作的七个时间阶段分析完成信号与应用语义满足之间的差距；2）对四个工业案例进行深入追踪：Meta的RoCE fabric、Google的1RMA重新设计、Microsoft的DCQCN失败、SDR-RDMA部分完成；3）对CXL 3.0、NVLink、UALink进行对比分析。

Result: 1）RDMA完成语义确实存在"完成谬误"——保证放置但不保证承诺；2）该差距可任意大，已有多个工业案例因此失败；3）CXL 3.0、NVLink、UALink均只能部分解决该问题，无法完全消除；4）只有具备强制反射阶段的协议架构才能彻底消除该差距。

Conclusion: RDMA的完成语义存在根本性的类别错误，现有技术均无法完全解决该问题。论文主张OAE链接状态机及其强制反射阶段是解决"完成谬误"的唯一方案。本文为该系列后续论文奠定基础，将进一步探讨解决方案的具体实现。

Abstract: This is the third of five papers comprising The Semantic Arrow of Time. Parts I and II identified computing's hidden semantic arrow of time, the FITO category mistake, and presented the constructive alternative: the OAE link state machine with its mandatory reflecting phase.
  This paper examines what happens when those principles are violated at industrial scale. Remote Direct Memory Access (RDMA) is the highest-performance data movement technology in production, deployed across Meta's 24,000-GPU clusters, Google's data centers, and Microsoft's Azure infrastructure. We argue that RDMA's completion semantics contain a category mistake: they guarantee placement (data written to a remote NIC buffer) but not commitment (data semantically integrated by the receiving application). We call this the completion fallacy.
  We document the fallacy through seven temporal stages of an RDMA Write operation, showing that the gap between completion signal and application semantic satisfaction can be arbitrarily large. We trace consequences through four case studies: Meta's RoCE fabric, Google's 1RMA redesign, Microsoft's DCQCN failures, and SDR-RDMA partial completions.
  A comparative analysis shows CXL 3.0, NVLink, and UALink each address parts of the completion fallacy but none eliminates it entirely. Only a protocol architecture with a mandatory reflecting phase can close the gap between delivery and commitment.

</details>


### [133] [Unlocking Python's Cores: Hardware Usage and Energy Implications of Removing the GIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04782)
*José Daniel Montoya Salazar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究评估了Python 3.14.2无GIL构建对性能、能效和硬件利用率的影响，针对四类工作负载进行实验。结果显示：并行工作负载可获得最高4倍加速和成比例的能耗降低，但内存占用增加；顺序工作负载能耗增加13-43%；频繁对象访问的工作负载因锁竞争收益降低或性能退化。无GIL构建并非通用优化方案，需根据工作负载特性评估是否适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的GIL阻止多线程在多核CPU上并行执行。Python 3.13开始实验性支持禁用GIL，但此前研究主要关注加速效果，对能耗和硬件利用率的影响缺乏关注。

Method: 使用四类工作负载（NumPy计算、顺序内核、线程化数值工作负载、线程化对象工作负载）对比Python 3.14.2的GIL和无GIL构建，测量执行时间、CPU利用率、内存占用和能耗。

Result: 并行工作负载：执行时间最多缩短4倍，能耗成比例降低，多核利用率良好，但内存占用增加；顺序工作负载：能耗增加13-43%；频繁共享对象访问的工作负载：因锁竞争导致收益降低或退化；内存方面：虚拟内存增加更明显，主要由于对象级锁、线程安全机制和新内存分配器。

Conclusion: Python无GIL构建不是通用优化方案。开发者采用前需评估工作负载是否适合并行执行。

Abstract: Python's Global Interpreter Lock prevents execution on more than one CPU core at the same time, even when multiple threads are used. However, starting with Python 3.13 an experimental build allows disabling the GIL. While prior work has examined speedup implications of this disabling, the effects on energy consumption and hardware utilization have received less attention. This study measures execution time, CPU utilization, memory usage, and energy consumption using four workload categories: NumPy-based, sequential kernels, threaded numerical workloads, and threaded object workloads, comparing GIL and free-threaded builds of Python 3.14.2.
  The results highlight a trade-off. For parallelizable workloads operating on independent data, the free-threaded build reduces execution time by up to 4 times, with a proportional reduction in energy consumption, and effective multi-core utilization, at the cost of an increase in memory usage. In contrast, sequential workloads do not benefit from removing the GIL and instead show a 13-43% increase in energy consumption. Similarly, workloads where threads frequently access and modify the same objects show reduced improvements or even degradation due to lock contention. Across all workloads, energy consumption is proportional to execution time, indicating that disabling the GIL does not significantly affect power consumption, even when CPU utilization increases. When it comes to memory, the no-GIL build shows a general increase, more visible in virtual memory than in physical memory. This increase is primarily attributed to per-object locking, additional thread-safety mechanisms in the runtime, and the adoption of a new memory allocator.
  These findings suggest that Python's no-GIL build is not a universal improvement. Developers should evaluate whether their workload can effectively benefit from parallel execution before adoption.

</details>


### [134] [PromptTuner: SLO-Aware Elastic System for LLM Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05087)
*Wei Gao,Peng Sun,Dmitrii Ustiugov,Tianwei Zhang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptTuner，一个用于优化LLM提示调优的SLO感知弹性系统，通过Prompt Bank和Workload Scheduler两项创新设计，显著降低SLO违规和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习资源管理系统不足以优化LLM提示调优工作负载，无法同时满足用户的服务水平目标（SLO）并降低资源配置成本。IT企业提供的Prompt-Tuning-as-a-Service服务需要解决这一挑战。

Method: PromptTuner采用两项创新设计：(1) Prompt Bank（提示库）用于识别高效的初始提示以加速提示调优的收敛；(2) Workload Scheduler（工作负载调度器）实现快速资源分配以减少SLO违规和资源成本。

Result: 实验评估表明，PromptTuner相比INFless和ElasticFlow分别将SLO违规降低4.0倍和7.9倍，成本降低1.6倍和4.5倍。

Conclusion: PromptTuner effectively optimizes LLM prompt tuning workloads by addressing resource management limitations, achieving substantial improvements in SLO compliance and cost reduction compared to existing systems.

Abstract: Prompt tuning has become a prominent strategy for enhancing the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) on downstream tasks. Many IT enterprises now offer Prompt-Tuning-as-a-Service to fulfill the growing demand for prompt tuning LLMs on downstream tasks. Their primary objective is to satisfy users Service Level Objectives (SLOs) while reducing resource provisioning costs. Nevertheless, our characterization analysis for existing deep learning resource management systems reveals that they are insufficient to optimize these objectives for LLM prompt tuning workloads.
  In this paper, we introduce PromptTuner, an SLO-aware elastic system to optimize LLM prompt tuning. It contains two innovations. (1) We design a Prompt Bank to identify efficient initial prompts to expedite the convergence of prompt tuning. (2) We develop aWorkload Scheduler to enable fast resource allocation to reduce the SLO violation and resource costs. In our evaluation, PromptTuner reduces SLO violations by 4.0x and 7.9x, and lowers costs by 1.6x and 4.5x, compared to INFless and ElasticFlow respectively.

</details>


### [135] [Radiation Hydrodynamics at Scale: Comparing MPI and Asynchronous Many-Task Runtimes with FleCSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05366)
*Alexander Strack,Hartmut Kaiser,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FleCSI框架提供高层编程接口，支持MPI和异步多任务运行时(AMTR)后端。MPI在131072核心上实现97%以上并行效率，HPX在少于64节点时性能优于MPI+Kokkos，但受限于非优化集体操作导致扩展性受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编写高效的分布式代码仍然是劳动密集型且复杂的任务，需要简化应用开发同时充分利用异步并行性。

Method: 使用FleCSI的三个后端(MPI、Legion、HPX)在最多1024个节点上对两个应用进行基准测试：简单泊松求解器和多维辐射流体动力学代码HARD。

Result: MPI后端在131072核心上实现97%以上并行效率；Legion后端存在明显开销和扩展性限制；HPX后端相比MPI+Kokkos开销极小，但在少于64节点时HPX性能更优(弱扩展1.31倍加速，强扩展1.27倍)；仅流体动力学测试中，少于32节点时HPX比MPI快1.20倍，比MPI+Kokkos快1.64倍。

Conclusion: FleCSI抽象层引入的开销极小。HPX后端在计算密集型工作负载上具有性能优势，但因非优化HPX集体操作导致扩展性受限。

Abstract: Writing efficient distributed code remains a labor-intensive and complex endeavor. To simplify application development, the Flexible Computational Science Infrastructure (FleCSI) framework offers a user-oriented, high-level programming interface that is built upon a task-based runtime model. Internally, FleCSI integrates state-of-the-art parallelization backends, including MPI and the asynchronous many-task runtimes (AMTRs) Legion and HPX, enabling applications to fully leverage asynchronous parallelism. In this work, we benchmark two applications using FleCSI's three backends on up to 1024 nodes, intending to quantify the advantages and overheads introduced by the AMTR backends. As representative applications, we select a simple Poisson solver and the multidimensional radiation hydrodynamics code HARD. In the communication-focused Poisson solver benchmark, FleCSI achieves over 97% parallel efficiency using the MPI backend under weak scaling on up to 131072 cores, indicating that only minimal overhead is introduced by its abstraction layer. While the Legion backend exhibits notable overheads and scaling limitations, the HPX backend introduces only marginal overhead compared to MPI+Kokkos. However, the scalability of the HPX backend is currently limited due to the usage of non-optimized HPX collective operations. In the computation-focused radiation hydrodynamics benchmarks, the performance gap between the MPI and HPX backends fades. On fewer than 64 nodes, the HPX backend outperforms MPI+Kokkos, achieving an average speedup of 1.31 under weak scaling and up to 1.27 under strong scaling. For the hydrodynamics-only HARD benchmark, the HPX backend demonstrates superior performance on fewer than 32 nodes, achieving speedups of up to 1.20 relative to MPI and up to 1.64 relative to MPI+Kokkos.

</details>
